# FEATHERFLOW — Live Browser Verification

Method: Playwright-core 1.63 driving installed Google Chrome 154 (headless) against the frontend at `http://127.0.0.1:8080/` (release build of the working tree, `API_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000`) and the backend recorded in `FEATHERFLOW_RUNTIME_TRUTH.md`. Each role used a **fresh browser context** (empty storage/service-worker/cache). The Flutter semantics (accessibility) tree was enabled so widgets could be located and clicked with real mouse events at their on-screen coordinates. Network responses for `http://127.0.0.1:8000/` were captured per click; console warnings/errors were captured.

Crawl procedure per role: login → (dismiss first-login language dialog) → capture dashboard screenshot → click every distinct visible button/tab/card (depth 0), and on each newly opened route click up to 8 distinct visible controls (depth 1) → return to the starting route between clicks → reload at the start route (session persistence) → 390px and 320px viewport screenshots → logout/relogin (separate pass, §3).
**Safety rule:** controls whose label matched `delete|remove|suspend|reject|cancel|clear|revoke|ban|block|disable|deactivate|terminate|logout|reset|purge|decline` were **not clicked** (recorded as "Skipped"). Consequently destructive/mutating workflows are *not* UI-verified here; they are covered at API/script level only and marked as such in the feature matrix. Controls that open a form/dialog were opened but forms were not submitted, except the dedicated farmer add-expense test (§4).
Screenshots: the Screenshot column names files from the full capture set (1,794 PNGs, ~135 MB, kept outside the repository). A key subset — every role's dashboard at 1366/390/320 px plus the evidence images cited below — is in `audit_logs/screenshots/`. Raw per-role crawl JSON is in `audit_logs/data/crawls/`, the harness in `audit_logs/tools/`.

(2) *Pharmacy owner (suspended)* lands on `/login` (F-002). (3) The `*(pending demo)*` accounts are approved/active (F-029). (4) Operations Admin's first crawl was invalidated by a harness mis-click and re-run (see §4); the table shows the re-run. (5) "Non-2xx" for the Super/Ops/Support/Doctor-Admin rows are all F-001.

## 3. Session lifecycle per role (reload → logout → protected route → relogin)

Pass criteria: after a browser reload at the role's home route the user stays signed in; the logout control returns to `/login`; opening the role's home route afterwards redirects to `/login`; logging in again lands on the same home route with the same data (volatile content such as clocks/activity feeds excluded). Harness note: the login form fields must be *typed* (`keyboard.type`), not `fill()`ed, after a logout/reload — a Playwright artefact, not a product fault.

| Role | Reload keeps session | Logout control | After logout | Home route when signed out | Relogin → route | Same view after relogin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Farmer | yes | Profile → "Sign out" (+ confirm dialog) | `/login` | `/login` | `/farmer` | yes |
| Doctor | yes | Profile → "Log Out" | `/login` | `/login` | `/doctor` | yes |
| Pharmacy owner | yes | AppBar "Sign out" | `/login` | `/login` | `/pharmacy` | yes |
| Delivery rider | yes | Profile tab → "Log Out" (verified in a separate run) | `/login` | `/login` | `/delivery` | yes |
| Researcher | yes | Sidebar "Sign Out" | `/login` | `/login` | `/research` | yes |
| Feed Inventory Owner | yes | "Sign out" (+ confirm) | `/login` | `/login` | `/feed-owner` | yes |
| Feed Admin | yes | Sidebar "Sign Out" | `/login` | `/login` | `/admin` | yes |
| Super / Operations / Finance / Content / Research / Delivery / Doctor / Pharmacy / Support / Team Admin | yes (10/10) | Sidebar "Sign Out" | `/login` | `/login` | `/admin` | yes for Super, Ops, Delivery, Pharmacy; "different text" for Finance, Content, Research, Doctor, Support, Team because the recent-activity/shift widgets change between logins — no data loss |

## 4. Targeted browser tests

| Test | Result | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| **Farmer add expense through the UI** (Cost Management → Add expense → amount 1234, supplier `AUDIT-UI-EXPENSE`) | `POST /api/farmers/costs/expenses/` → **201**; row present via API with `category Feed, amount 1234.0, supplier_name AUDIT-UI-EXPENSE, payment_status pending`; list reachable after full page reload; row then deleted (204). First text input = amount, second = supplier | `exp_filled.png`, `exp_after.png` |
| **Role-guard deep links** (8 + 3 + 4 + 3 routes) | server returned 403 everywhere, but the UI opened every shell (R1/F-007). Farmer → `#/doctor`: Doctor panel with **demo numbers** and no error (F-008). Farmer → `#/admin/users`: "Back · User Management · … An active admin account is required." | `guard_*.png`, `guard_results.json` |
| **Pharmacist (legacy role) login** | lands in Farmer shell, banner "A farmer account is required." + Retry, 62 × 403 (F-006) | `pharmacist_dashboard.png` |
| **Suspended pharmacy login** | "Login Successful! Opening your dashboard…" then back on `/login`, credentials still in the form, no message (F-002) | `susp_pharm_700/1500/3000.png` |
| **Pending-labelled demo accounts** (`pharmacy.pending.agrocare`, `dr.pending.kabir`, `rider.pending.belal`, `research.pending.mahmud`) | all open their normal panels: the demo accounts are actually approved/active (`/api/pharmacy/permissions/` → `ACTIVE, review_state approved`) so the **pending/locked UI was not observed live** (F-029) | `ph_pending_home.png` |
| **Admin Doctors & Patients** | "No doctors found" + toast "Admin request failed (500)" (F-001) | `admin_doctor_d0_21.png` |
| **Feed Owner → Community** | opens the community feed (200) incl. a Feed Marketplace Admin announcement (`#marketplaceannouncements`) | `fo_community.png` |
| **Operations Admin → "Oversight"** | first run signed the user out; root cause was a **harness mis-click** (the sidebar item sits under the Sign-Out footer region); re-run with that region excluded: 188 controls, 0 anomalies other than the F-001 500s | `crawl_admin_operations_run1.json` vs rerun |
| **Responsive** (landing screens at 390 px and 320 px for every role) | screenshots captured (`<role>_w390.png`, `<role>_w320.png`); reviewed Super Admin, Doctor, Pharmacy: layouts reflow (hamburger menu, chips wrap), no clipped primary actions seen; Super Admin AppBar title truncates to "D…" at 320 px (cosmetic) | screenshots |
| **Console** | no JS exceptions; only Flutter "Noto fonts" warning and filtered Google-Maps loading notice | `crawl_*.json` |

## 5. Not verified in a browser (do **not** read the matrix as "working")

* Every signup wizard (`/signup/*`), OTP verification and password-reset completion.
* All create/edit/delete **forms** except farmer add-expense: revenue, loan, flock, labour payments, medicine/product creation, community post, paper submission, booking dialog, checkout (feed/pharmacy/subscription), disease image upload, proof-photo upload.
* All state-changing buttons (approve/suspend/cancel/assign/refund/resolve/accept/complete/sign-out/start-shift) were excluded by the crawl rule — except those clicked by mistake (fault register S-1…S-4).
* Real-time features: Socket.IO chat, Jitsi video, live rider location, map tiles.
* Offline/slow-network behaviour; mobile (Android/iOS) builds; non-Chrome browsers; service-worker upgrade path.
* The seven roles' *locked/pending* UI states (no demo account is actually pending).

## 6. Side effects of this browser audit

See `FEATHERFLOW_FAULT_REGISTER.md` §"Audit-induced side effects" S-1…S-9 (shifts started then ended, riders toggled online then restored, 4 queued delivery orders cancelled, 1 content report dismissed, 1 appointment marked no-show, notifications marked read).
